			function readCookieDelit(name) {
				var name_cook = name + "=";
				var spl = document.cookie.split(';');
				for(var i=0; i<spl.length; i++) {
					var c = spl[i];
					while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
					if(c.indexOf(name_cook) == 0) return c.substring(name_cook.length, c.length);
				}
				return null;
			}
